# 课文英文翻译

# English Translations of the Texts

#### Lesson 1 我们去机场接你们

##### 课文1

Liu Ming: Is that Yang Tongle? Why is he with Bai Jiayue?

Wang Yixue: That's not Yang Tongle. That's Li Wen. He's a good friend of Bai Jiayue.

Liu Ming: Oh, I thought it was Tongle. They do look a bit alike.

Wang Yixue: Yes, they look somewhat alike, but he's taller than Tongle—about 1.8 meters.

Liu Ming: And he looks a bit thinner, too.

Wang Yixue: Jiayue and Li Wen are coming to Beijing next week. I’m going to pick them up at the airport.

Liu Ming: What day will they get here? I'll come with you to pick them up.

##### 课文2

Bai Jiayue: Why hasn't my luggage shown up yet? Could it be lost?

Li Wen: What does your suitcase look like?

Bai Jiayue: It's a black one with my name and phone number on it.

Li Wen: It seems I've seen one like that somewhere. Could someone have taken it by mistake?

Bai Jiayue: Let's quickly find someone to ask.

There are quite a few important things in it.

Li Wen: Don't worry. Let's take our passports and plane tickets to the service desk and ask there.

##### 课文3

Liu Ming: The plane arrived a while ago. Have you seen Bai Jiayue and Li Wen?

Wang Yixue: No, they should be coming out any minute now.

Liu Ming: Let's stand in the middle so it'll be easier for them to spot us.

Wang Yixue: Look, is that tall guy Li Wen?

Liu Ming: Which one do you mean? The young man with short hair, wearing black?

Wang Yixue: Yes, that's Li Wen! And look,

Jiayue is right behind him.

##### 课文4

Yesterday I arrived in Beijing. Though the journey took more than ten hours, sitting next to Jiayue made the time pass quickly. When we arrived at the airport, Jiayue found her suitcase missing. So I took her to the service desk and helped her retrieve it. By the time we left the airport, it was already past 8 p.m. As soon as we stepped outside, we saw Ms. Wang's cousin, who looked younger than in her photos. In the evening, Yixue took us to a Chinese restaurant with amazing food.

#### Lesson 2 你们想吃什么就点什么

##### 课文1

Wang Yixue: Jiayue, Li Wen, take a look at the menu. What do you feel like eating?

Li Wen: Thank you, Yixue. I'm good with anything—why don't you go ahead and order?

Bai Jiayue: After such a long flight, I'm really

hungry and thirsty.

Wang Yixue: Then let's order plenty—don't be shy. What would you like to drink?

Bai Jiayue: It's been ages since I've had Chinese tea or jiaozi. I'd like some green tea and jiaozi, if that's okay.

Wang Yixue: No problem. Have another look and see if there's anything else you'd like.

##### 课文2

Wang Yixue: Waitress, could you please bring another pair of chopsticks, a spoon, and a bowl? Waitress: Certainly. Please wait a moment—I'll bring them right away.

Wang Yixue: The service here is always warm and welcoming, and all the dishes are delicious. Give it a try and let me know what you think! Bai Jiayue: Every dish is delicious. The dishes you ordered are really great.

Wang Yixue: The food here is both affordable and tasty. We come here so often that the staff already know our favorite dishes.

Waitress: Ms. Wang, since you're such a regular customer, we'd like to offer you some complimentary fruit today. Please enjoy! definitely come back for it again.

Bai Jiayue: This chicken rice is so delicious! I'll Li Wen: You can take this menu with you and check what else you might want to order next time.

##### 课文3

Wang Yixue: No need to take the menu. You can check it on your phone and even place an order for delivery.

Bai Jiayue: They do delivery here?

Wang Yixue: Yes! Many restaurants offer delivery these days. You can order whatever you feel like.

Bai Jiayue: That's really convenient!

##### 课文4

Over the years I spent abroad, I rarely cooked for myself. For breakfast, I would usually have a piece of bread, sometimes a slice of cake, and a cup of coffee. I ate both lunch and dinner out. During exam periods, when I was busy and exhausted, with no time to eat out and nothing at home, I could go for days surviving only on instant noodles. Now that I'm back in China, being able to enjoy my mom's cooking every day makes me incredibly happy! Even when she prepares just a few simple dishes, I love them all the same, because nothing in the world tastes better than a meal made by Mom.

#### Lesson 3 这个小区挺好的

##### 课文1

Wang Yixue: Since Xiaoxue's middle school is quite far from home, let's find a place to live that's closer.

Liu Ming: Sounds good. I'll check online. What do you think about this neighborhood?

Wang Yixue: The surroundings look quite nice, and it's not far from the subway station.

Liu Ming: The inside of the apartment looks good, too. The air conditioner and washing machine are both new.

Wang Yixue: It is nice, but I'm not a fan of living on the first floor.

Liu Ming: This first-floor unit has a small garden. I think we should go and take a look anyway.

##### 课文2

Wang Yixue: When I came in this morning, the lights were still on.

Liu Ming: I've been swamped these days. I probably forgot to turn them off when I left. Wang Yixue: The fridge isn't working. Where should I put all this food?

Liu Ming: That's impossible! It's brand new. Let me take a look.

Wang Yixue: Is the washing machine broken, too?

Liu Ming: No, it's fine. But the bathroom hasn't been cleaned yet, so we can't do the laundry.

Wang Yixue: Will we be able to move in this Sunday?

##### 课文3

Wang Yixue: We've spent quite a lot of money this month.

Liu Ming: Yes, we bought a lot of things for the move.

Wang Yixue: Let's get a credit card. That way, we can pay it off over time. I heard Bank of China offers good service, and we can even get discounts when shopping with their credit card. Liu Ming: Great! There's a Bank of China branch nearby—just a few minutes' walk from here. When should we go?

Wang Yixue: How about this afternoon?

Liu Ming: I have to go to the hospital this afternoon. I probably won't be back until late.

Wang Yixue: Then I'll go by myself this afternoon.

##### 课文4

Liu Ming: I think we can move this week. I'll buy some cardboard boxes tomorrow, and we'll be ready in no time. On moving day, I'd like to ask a few friends to help. We can probably finish everything within a day.

Wang Yixue: I don't think we can move until next month. We can start preparing this week and clean the new place next week. With so much to do, we'll need at least two weeks to get everything done. On moving day, we should hire a moving company.

##### Lesson 4

#### 这家宾馆跟别的都不一样

##### 课文1

Yang Tongle: How about going somewhere for a vacation?

Wang Yifei: Sure, we can go wherever you'd like.

Yang Tongle: You like the sea—how about

staying by the coast for a few days?

Wang Yifei: It's a bit too cold to go to the

seaside now.

Yang Tongle: Then let's go to the grasslands. It's not cold there at all.

Wang Yifei: Great idea! I haven't ridden a horse in ages.

Yang Tongle: Exactly! Riding horses, eating mutton, and watching the moon on the grasslands must be wonderful.

##### 课文2

Yang Tongle: Have you booked the plane tickets for our trip?

Wang Yifei: Yes, I have. The flight departs at a quarter past ten on Saturday morning.

Yang Tongle: Have you chosen the hotel as well?

Wang Yifei: Yes. This hotel is really special—it's unlike any other. The moment you step outside, you can see cows and sheep!

Yang Tongle: Perfect! Let's check what we need to take.

Wang Yifei: We don't need to bring too much. Just don't forget the new camera we bought. Yang Tongle: I definitely won't. I'll go back right now.

##### 课文3

Xiao Li: Welcome! I'm your driver. My last name is Li—just call me Xiao Li.

Yang Tongle: Hello! I'm sorry our flight was delayed, and you've been waiting for a long time. Xiao Li: That's all right. Besides this suitcase, do you have anything else?

Yang Tongle: Just one more bag—I can carry it myself.

Xiao Li: The car is on the first floor. Please follow me.

Yang Tongle: Is our hotel far from the airport?

Xiao Li: Not far—we'll get there in about thirty minutes. Once you arrive, you can take a short rest first. I'll call you when it's time for dinner.

##### 课文4

Today is the first day of our trip. We got

up very early to go to the airport, but to our surprise, the flight was delayed. By the time we arrived, it was already dark. Fortunately, our driver, Xiao Li, had been waiting for us at the airport all along and wasn't the least bit impatient. He was extremely warm and friendly, telling us a lot about the local area along the way and even preparing some fruit for us. The hotel room is just as beautiful as I imagined—spacious and clean. I'm very satisfied with it.

#### Lesson 5 这样的照片才好看

##### 课文1

Bai Jiayue: It's been cloudy all week, but it's sunny today.

Li Wen: The weather is perfect now! How about going hiking?

Bai Jiayue: Great idea! We can get some exercise and take some nice photos.

Li Wen: I'll bring some water and snacks. Shall we go now?

Bai Jiayue: I'll go back to change into my sneakers and grab my overcoat. Should we invite Yixue to join us?

Li Wen: Good idea. Let's give her a call.

##### 课文2

Bai Jiayue: Who took these photos for you?

Every one of them looks great.

Wang Yixue: Yifei. She's always been interested in photography and often takes pictures of me.

Bai Jiayue: I really like this one where you're laughing—you look so pretty.

Wang Yixue: I think this one is a bit unflattering. I didn't realize she was taking a picture of me at the time.

Bai Jiayue: That's exactly what makes it so great! I like taking photos too. How about I take some pictures of you when we go hiking?

Wang Yixue: Great! Then I can compare which of you two has the better photography skills.

##### 课文3

Wang Yixue: Jiayue, it's really beautiful here. Let's take some pictures at this spot. Bai Jiayue: There's no sunlight here. Let's go over there instead. Wang Yixue: Alright. What do you think of me standing among these trees? Bai Jiayue: That looks good. Wait a moment—two people are walking behind you. Wang Yixue: I'm ready. Tell me when you're going to take the photo. Bai Jiayue: You don't have to look at me. Just do whatever feels natural. Wang Yixue: Some birds just flew onto the tree. I'll watch them then. Bai Jiayue: Oh no, the phone's out of battery.

##### 课文4

Jiayue, I received your email and saw the photos you took of me—every single one is beautiful. I've already told Yifei that your photography skills are better than hers, and she's quite upset about it. Haha. I'm wondering if you're interested in Chinese music. I have a ticket for tomorrow afternoon's concert for you. The concert will finish a little after 7 p.m. Once it's over, come to my place for dinner and try some of my homemade Chinese dishes. What do you think?

# Lesson 6 高铁上还可以点外卖

##### 课文1

Li Wen: Jiayue, it's time to buy our tickets to Shanghai. How do you want to get there? Bai Jiayue: I've never taken a high-speed train before. How about we go by high-speed train? Li Wen: No problem. There are many high-speed trains from Beijing to Shanghai—it's very convenient.

Bai Jiayue: Great! How do we buy the tickets? Li Wen: We can buy them on a mobile App. Give me your passport, and I'll get them for you. Bai Jiayue: I've heard about the high-speed train for a long time. I'm glad I can finally take it. Li Wen: It's fast and comfortable—you're going to love it.

##### 课文2

Liu Ming: Once we pass the intersection up ahead, we'll reach the high-speed train station. Bai Jiayue: I see it. We'll be there soon. There are a lot of cars on this road. Please drive carefully. Liu Ming: Okay. You still have an hour, so you probably won't be late. Bai Jiayue: I've noticed that this road not only has heavy traffic but also many traffic lights. Liu Ming: Yeah, I took this road once before and never used it again afterward. Bai Jiayue: Then why did you take it today? Liu Ming: I got anxious and took a wrong turn. If I hadn't, we would've arrived twenty minutes earlier.

Bai Jiayue: Li Wen, my earphones have run out of battery. Do you have a power bank?

Li Wen: Yes, I keep all these daily essentials in my bag. Let me get it for you.

Bai Jiayue: There are more and more people ahead of us.

Li Wen: Right, we're about to enter the high-speed train station. Let's stay together and don't get separated.

Bai Jiayue: Okay, could you check if we've got all the high-speed train tickets?

Li Wen: No need for a paper ticket. You can just scan your passport to enter.

Bai Jiayue: Where do we do the ticket check?

Li Wen: The ticket gates are on the second floor. We'll take the elevator up in a moment.

##### 课文3

##### 课文4

During the vacation, I went to Beijing and also visited Shanghai. I took the high-speed train from Beijing South Railway Station to Shanghai. Sitting on the train felt just like sitting on my sofa at home. It's quiet and comfortable. When I got tired of sitting, I stood up for a while; when I got hungry, I ordered takeout. Yes, you heard that right—you can order takeout on a high-speed train! Just select your station and the food you want, and you don't need to get off the train when it arrives—the staff will bring it to you. Now, more and more people prefer traveling by high-speed train. When my parents come to China for a visit, I'll definitely take them on a high-speed train ride.

##### Lesson 7

#### 那条裙子比短裤更好看

##### 课文1

Wang Yixue: We bought this bicycle when Xiaoxue was eight years old. It's starting to look a bit old now.

Liu Ming: Yeah, she's grown taller, and the bike is too small for her now.

Wang Yixue: A new bicycle shop just opened in the mall. Let's get her a new one.

Liu Ming: Sure. Let's take Xiaoxue with us so she can try it out.

Wang Yixue: Let's go today, then! I also want to check out some clothes. How should we get there?

Liu Ming: The mall isn't far—we can walk there.

##### 课文2

Wang Yixue: Do you think these yellow shorts look good on me?

Liu Ming: The size fits well, but I think that skirt looks better than the shorts.

Wang Yixue: The skirt is a bit more expensive than the shorts.

Liu Ming: How much more expensive?

Wang Yixue: The skirt is 480 yuan, and the shorts are 400 yuan.

Liu Ming: The skirt isn't much more expensive than the shorts. Let's get the skirt—you look better in skirts.

Wang Yixue: I'll look around a bit more before deciding. Look, there's a "Buy Two, Get One Free" offer over there!

Store Clerk: These watermelons are big and fresh. Free if they aren't sweet!

Wang Yixue: The watermelons look great. How much are they?

##### 课文3

Store Clerk: Five yuan per kilo. Here, try this piece of chilled watermelon—it's super sweet!

Wang Yixue: It really is sweet! Please help me pick a bigger one, and I'll take two jin of bananas as well.

Store Clerk: That's 58.5 yuan in total.

Wang Yixue: I'm buying so much. Can you give me a discount?

Store Clerk: Make it 55 yuan. That's the best I can do.

##### 课文4

These past few days, while watching TV in our new home, we discovered another problem. Now that the house is bigger, the TV looks a bit too small. We bought it when we got married, so I think it's time for an upgrade. I've been shopping for TVs online all day, and to my surprise, they're not only more affordable but also much smarter—some even turn on when you talk to them. I found several I really like, but I'll let Yixue make the final call tonight.

#### Lesson 8 今天我出院了

Li Wen: Tianzhong, I've seen you at the gym quite often lately.

Chen Tianzhong: I come here to run for an hour every afternoon.

Li Wen: That's a great exercise habit.

Chen Tianzhong: I’ve gained more than ten jin this year. I really can’t afford to get any fatter—it’s not very healthy.

Li Wen: I often play badminton after class. Why don't you join us sometime?

Chen Tianzhong: I know, but you guys are way too good. I'm not very good at it.

Li Wen: That's okay. I can teach you.

##### 课文2

Annie: What's wrong? You don't look very well. Chen Tianzhong: My ear has been hurting since I went swimming yesterday.

Annie: Maybe you've caught a cold. Do you have a fever?

Chen Tianzhong: I think I have a mild fever.

Annie: Let me take you to the hospital so the doctor can check it.

Chen Tianzhong: I'll go home and take a nap first. Maybe I'll feel better after some rest.

Annie: Alright, but if you still have a fever this afternoon, you must see a doctor.

Chen Tianzhong: Thanks for your concern. I'll take care of myself.

##### 课文3

Annie: Why were you suddenly hospitalized? Everyone’s really worried about you.

Chen Tianzhong: My leg has been hurting for weeks. The doctor said I need to stay in the hospital for some tests.

Annie: You don't look like a patient at all.

Chen Tianzhong: I know, I rarely get sick—the last time I came to the hospital was nearly two years ago.

Annie: You run every day and sometimes go swimming. Could it be that you've been exercising too much?

Chen Tianzhong: The doctor thinks that might be the case, but we will have to wait for the test results to be sure.

Annie: Don't worry! Just follow the doctor's advice and get plenty of rest—your leg will soon recover.

Chen Tianzhong: Thanks. I always feel much happier when someone comes to visit me.

##### 课文4

Today I was discharged from the hospital. Before leaving, the doctor prescribed several kinds of medicine and explained how to take them. One needs to be taken once every night before going to bed, while the others should be taken three times a day after meals. In addition to taking medicine, the doctor told me that getting plenty of rest is the most important. I've kept that in mind and will take good care of myself after returning home. I hope my leg gets better soon.

#### Lesson 9 打不好没关系

##### 课文1

Li Wen: Jiayue, I'm sorry I forgot to return your campus card yesterday.

Bai Jiayue: That's OK. How did you get here so quickly?

Li Wen: I was playing basketball on the court.

As soon as you called, I ran right over.

Bai Jiayue: I heard that you guys have been practicing every day for the sports meet.

Li Wen: Yes, we have! Are you planning to take part in it?

Bai Jiayue: I'm thinking about joining the tennis competition. I've been practicing a lot lately.

Li Wen: Great! I'll come and watch your match then.

##### 课文2

Li Wen: Jiayue, why don't you come and play badminton with us?

Bai Jiayue: I haven't played in years. I've almost forgotten how.

Li Wen: It's not a competition. Don't worry if you're not good at it.

Bai Jiayue: But there are so many classmates playing, I'd feel embarrassed if I kept missing the shuttlecock.

Li Wen: You're overthinking it! We're just playing for exercise.

Bai Jiayue: I’d better practice on my own first.

I’ll join you guys next week.

Li Wen: I'll be playing badminton with Tianzhong. You can come and practice with us.

##### 课文3

Chen Tianzhong: The soccer game has started!

Hurry up and come watch!

Li Wen: You guys go ahead and watch. There's some beer and drinks in the fridge—I'll grab them.

Bai Jiayue: What's wrong with them today?

They just can't seem to score!

Chen Tianzhong: Several veteran players are sick. The new players are too nervous—it's their first big game.

Li Wen: Yeah, since the main players aren't playing, everyone else seems affected.

Bai Jiayue: The more I watch, the more anxious I feel. I'm done watching. Just tell me the score later.

##### 课文4

I love watching sporting events, and my favorite is the Olympic Games. The Olympics are the most influential sporting event in the world. From 1896 to 2024, each edition of the Games has attracted a huge number of athletes from around the globe. The first Chinese athlete to take part in the Olympics was Liu Changchun, who competed in the 100 m and 200 m sprint races in 1932. Although he didn't achieve great results, he helped introduce China to the world.

#### Lesson 10 你明天再把书还给我

##### 课文1

Classmate: Your math is getting better and better. Could you share your study methods? Liu Xiaoxue: Nothing special—I just pay close attention in class and take detailed notes. Classmate: I've seen your notes—they're really clear and well-organized. Liu Xiaoxue: The questions the teacher writes on the blackboard are all important, so I make sure to write them all down in my notebook. Classmate: You're absolutely right. I don't really have the habit of taking notes. Liu Xiaoxue: Notes are really important. I review them once before doing my homework every day. Classmate: That's such a great method! I'll try

Classmate: That's such a great method! I'll try it too—hopefully it'll help improve my grades.

##### 课文2

Classmate: Xiaoxue, how did you do on yesterday's exam?

Liu Xiaoxue: I thought the history exam was a bit difficult. What about you?

Classmate: On the math test, I didn't read the instructions carefully and got several questions wrong. I didn't do very well.

Liu Xiaoxue: There's another exam tomorrow—don't dwell on it too much. Let's focus on reviewing the foreign language for now.

Classmate: I have a few questions about the foreign language homework. Can I ask you for help?

Liu Xiaoxue: Of course! Feel free to ask me anything related to studying.

Liu Xiaoxue: Sorry, I don't know how to solve these questions either. Let's go to Ms. Li's office together and ask her for help.

##### 课文3

Classmate: Ms. Li, we have a few questions from the textbook that we'd like to ask you. Ms. Li: Sure, have a seat. Which questions are you struggling with?

Classmate: I don't understand this dialogue on page 5, and Xiaoxue doesn't get it either.

Ms. Li: I went over these sentences in class—let me explain them again.

(Ms. Li explains the questions to the students.)

Classmate: I finally get it now. These questions aren't difficult at all!

Liu Xiaoxue: I understand too. Thank you, Ms. Li.

Ms. Li: There are a few more exercises in this book of mine. Try working on them at home, and return the book to me tomorrow.

##### 课文4

Dear Sister Jiayue,

How have you been lately? Are you busy these days?

I’m now in my second year of middle school, and my studies are becoming more and more demanding. The year after next, I’ll be taking the high school entrance exams. In China, schools generally consist of six years of elementary school, three years of middle school, and three years of high school. My mom says that to get into a good university, you first need to attend a good high school—and to enter a

good high school, you need to have strong

grades in middle school. That's why I'm

working very hard now.

Did you know that, Sister Jiayue? Thanks to your influence, I've started to enjoy photography. Next time you visit Beijing, I'll show you some of the photos I've taken.

#### Lesson 11 看来我没办法解决这个问题

##### 课文1

Wang Yixue: What time does the meeting start this afternoon?

Yang Tongle: The manager isn't available this afternoon, so the meeting has been canceled.

Wang Yixue: Then when will it be held?

Yang Tongle: At 10 a.m. the day after tomorrow. The location has been changed to Meeting Room 1. I'm about to send everyone an email.

Wang Yixue: For the meeting, should we use the room's computer or bring our own?

Yang Tongle: Either works.

Wang Yixue: Let's go a bit earlier then—can you help me set up the computer?

##### 课文2

Wang Yixue: Is the computer set up?

Yang Tongle: It's already set up. Go ahead and turn it on to see if there's any issue.

Wang Yixue: I can't see anything at the moment.

Yang Tongle: Let me check what's wrong...

Try again.

Wang Yixue: Now I can see, but there's no sound.

Yang Tongle: How about now?

Wang Yixue: I can hear it now, but it's still too quiet to hear clearly.

Yang Tongle: Looks like I can't fix this problem.

We'll have to ask someone else for help.

##### 课文3

Wang Yixue: Tongle, have you seen Lao Zhang?

Yang Tongle: He didn't come in today. He said he had some family matters to take care of, so he took the day off.

Wang Yixue: Quite a few colleagues are on leave—I'm worried I won't be able to finish all this work before the meeting.

Yang Tongle: It's already past twelve. Let's grab some lunch first. After we eat, I'll help you finish these tasks.

Wang Yixue: You go ahead—I'll keep working for a bit longer. I need to send this to the company's email address before tomorrow's meeting.

Yang Tongle: Didn't you see the email? The manager isn't back yet, so the meeting's been postponed to next week.

Wang Yixue: That's a relief! Let's go eat.

##### 课文4

A few days ago, some colleagues from Shanghai visited Beijing. During dinner, they told me that many coworkers had recently moved to Shanghai for work and asked if I'd be willing to go there too. Here's how I feel about it: Right now, my husband and children are in Beijing. Liu Ming's job is very demanding, and Xiaoxue and Xiaoming are still young. If I were to work in another city, I'd have to leave them behind. While Shanghai might offer better career opportunities, life itself is just as important to me. I'm not willing to leave my family behind for work or study.

# Lesson 12 这个季节天气变化很快

##### 课文1

Bai Jiayue: Ms. Wang, look! The trees along this street are in bloom.

Wang Yifei: Last week, I went boating in the park—the flowers there were blooming, too. Bai Jiayue: The weather is so nice. I'd like to go boating in the park this afternoon.

Wang Yifei: Go ahead! It's a weekday, so there probably won't be many people.

Bai Jiayue: Do you have time? I'd like to go with you.

Wang Yifei: I have classes this afternoon, so I can't go too far.

Bai Jiayue: Then shall we go another day?

Wang Yifei: Sure—either tomorrow or the day after. I'll give you a call.

##### 课文2

How did it suddenly get so windy?

Wang Yifei: I think the news said it's going to rain today.

Bai Jiayue: Really? I usually keep an umbrella in my bag, but I didn't bring it today.

Wang Yifei: I can lend you one—I have both an umbrella and a raincoat in my car.

Bai Jiayue: Thanks! I hope the weather clears up this afternoon.

Wang Yifei: The weather changes quickly at this time of year.

Bai Jiayue: It's already starting to rain—let's hurry and go.

##### 课文3

Bai Jiayue: You've been here for over a year now—have you gotten used to it?

Wang Yifei: I've gotten used to most things, but I'm still not quite used to the weather.

Bai Jiayue: Why? Don't you like the weather here?

Wang Yifei: I think the winters are too cold.

Bai Jiayue: Winter may be cold, but it's especially beautiful when it snows.

Wang Yifei: I like snow too. It used to snow often in Beijing, but I heard it only snowed once this year.

Bai Jiayue: I haven't kept up with Beijing's weather, but the news said that many places have had less snow this winter.

##### 课文4

It's been over a year since I left Beijing to work here. After living here for so long, I still prefer Beijing's four distinct seasons. Spring in Beijing is the most pleasant—it's neither too cold nor too hot. Summer days can be scorching, but evenings are pleasantly cool, which is why many people prefer going out after sunset. In autumn, the leaves turn vibrant shades of red and yellow, creating stunning views. Winter, however, is not only bitterly cold but also extremely windy, making it my least favorite season. Everyone has their own favorite season—which one do you like the most?

#### Lesson 13 我的新邻居来自英国

##### 课文1

Yang Tongle: Today's my treat—feel free to order anything you like!

Wang Yixue: Then I won't hold back. I've heard the fish at this restaurant is quite special.

Yang Tongle: That's right. Southern dishes are prepared differently from northern ones.

Besides fish, is there anything else you'd like?

Colleague: I don't often eat southern food—why don't you decide?

Yang Tongle: Okay, let's add a chicken dish and a...

Wang Yixue: There are only three of us—no need to order so much.

Yang Tongle: Got it. Let's start with these dishes for now.

Wang Yixue: Oh, by the way, my son's birthday is next week. If you're free, come over to my place—I'll cook some Beijing dishes for you.

##### 课文2

Wang Yixue: It's our first time having the new neighbors over—I keep worrying that we might have missed something.

Liu Ming: Don't worry, I've double-checked everything. We're all set.

Wang Yixue: I thought I already took out the drinks—where did they go?

Liu Ming: I put all the drinks and alcohol in the fridge. We'll take them out when the guests arrive.

Wang Yixue: Does the music volume sound okay?

Liu Ming: You've turned it up too loud. I'll lower it a bit.

Wang Yixue: Alright, let's wait for the guests now.

##### 课文3

Wang Yixue: Thank you all for coming to my son's birthday party. I hope everyone has a wonderful time tonight.

Yang Tongle: Happy birthday, Xiaoming! I brought a small gift for you—I hope you like it! Liu Xiaoming: Thank you! May I open it now? Yang Tongle: Of course. Your mom mentioned that you love painting, just like your sister. Is that right?

Liu Xiaoming: Yes, I want to be a painter someday. Wow—a drawing board and paintbrushes. This is exactly what I wanted. I love it so much!

Wang Yixue: I'll go get the plates. How about we have some cake and chat for a while?

##### 课文4

My new neighbors are a couple from the United Kingdom. Last week, I visited their home. They prepared a lot of delicious food and even served British tea. That day, we chatted over tea until after midnight. When they were young, both of them loved traveling. There's a world map in their home, and each red dot on the map represents a place they've visited. Later, they became fascinated by Eastern culture and started learning Chinese. Today is Xiaoming's birthday, and I've invited them to my home to experience a Chinese household and try some Chinese cuisine.

#### Lesson 14 这本书被别人借走了

##### 课文1

Li Wen: The dictionary I borrowed is due today, but I don't have time to return it.

Bai Jiayue: I only have two classes this afternoon, so I can help you return it to the library.

Li Wen: Thank you so much! Could you also help me borrow another book?

Bai Jiayue: Sure, just tell me the title.

Li Wen: I'll write it down for you. I've been to the library several times, but this book is always checked out.

book—I'll lend it to you tomorrow.

Bai Jiayue: Stories of Celebrities? I have that

##### 课文2

Annie: Classes ended early today. What are your plans for later?

Bai Jiayue: I'm really busy today. First, I need to go to the library to borrow some books, and then I'm going swimming.

Annie: Let's go together. I want to check out some Chinese newspapers at the library.

Bai Jiayue: Wait a minute—my campus card is missing. I won't be able to enter the library.

without it.

Annie: Do you remember when you last used it?

Bai Jiayue: I don't remember. Maybe I left it at home.

Annie: It's okay if you can't get into the library.

You can look for e-books online instead.

Bai Jiayue: No, I have to go—I have to return

Li Wen's book.

##### 课文3

Li Wen: There's a school party next week. Have you heard about it?

Bai Jiayue: Yes, a few other girls and I are going to perform. Are you joining too?

Li Wen: I want to sing a Chinese song, but I haven't found anyone to perform with yet. I'm not sure what to do.

Bai Jiayue: If you can't find anyone, just perform solo.

Li Wen: Just thinking about performing in front

Li Wen: Just thinking about performing in front of the principal, teachers, and so many classmates makes me a bit nervous.

Bai Jiayue: What's there to be nervous about?

We all love hearing you sing. You just need to believe in yourself.

##### 课文4

Yesterday was our school's New Year's party, and students from every class put on performances. The party started at 8 p.m. and didn't end until nearly midnight. I spent the whole evening singing and dancing with everyone—it was so much fun, but I was completely worn out. My favorite act was the dance by the international students. Toward the end, even the teachers and the principal were invited to join the dance floor, which made it especially entertaining. In a few days, the videos will be uploaded to the school's website, and I'm planning to watch them again.

#### Lesson 15 我是半个南京人

##### 课文1

Yang Tongle: You've been living in the Lantian Neighborhood for a while now. How do you like it?

Wang Yixue: The surroundings are quite nice, and it's very convenient for shopping. There's a supermarket right across the street.

Yang Tongle: Where do the kids usually play?

Wang Yixue: There are plenty of trees in the community, so after school, the kids play games under them. Are you thinking about buying a house?

Yang Tongle: Yes, I want to buy a big house so my parents can come and live with me.

Wang Yixue: There are quite a few elderly people in Lantian Neighborhood, and in recent years the community has made many changes to suit them.

##### 课文2

Yang Tongle: That sounds great! I'll go check out Lantian Neighborhood in a few days. Wang Yixue: I'm thinking of going to Nanjing for the vacation, but I don't know much about it. Yang Tongle: Although I'm not from Nanjing, I went to university there, so I'm basically half a local.

Wang Yixue: That's great! Could you tell me more about Nanjing?

Yang Tongle: Nanjing has a history of over 2000 years. It's got ancient city walls, historic streets, and famous landmarks. It attracts countless tourists every year.

Wang Yixue: Besides those popular tourist spots, do you have any recommendations for hidden gems?

Yang Tongle: There are so many! It would take a while to list them all.

Wang Yixue: Sounds good. Let me buy you dinner after work, and we can chat over a meal.

##### 课文3

Wang Yixue: Xiaoming, there is a major river in China named after a color. Do you know which river it is?

Liu Xiaoming: I know! It's the Yellow River! Wang Yixue: That's right. Here's another question—do you know why it's called the Yellow River?

Liu Xiaoming: It's because the water looks yellow! By the way, is the Yellow River the longest river in China?

Wang Yixue: No, the Yellow River isn't the longest, but it is historically very important.

Liu Xiaoming: I know! A long time ago, many Chinese people lived along the Yellow River. Wang Yixue: Exactly. To us Chinese, the Yellow River is like a mother—it has nurtured us for thousands of years.

##### 课文4

When I was little, I lived in the northwest part of Beijing, where a very famous river flowed by. My home was right by the river. There were tall trees and lush green grass all around. In spring, flowers along the riverbank would bloom, making the scenery especially beautiful. I still remember that every summer, I would go swimming in the river. As I grew older, whenever I felt unhappy, I would sit under the trees by the river, listening to the birds, watching the flowing water, and chatting with friends I met there. My sadness would melt away in no time. That's my favorite place.

#### Lesson 16

#### 我听说有的熊猫出国了

##### 课文1

Liu Xiaoxue: How long have you been taking care of this kitten?

Staff: Over a year now. When we first found it, it was dirty and tiny.

Liu Xiaoxue: Now it's so clean and beautiful! Staff: Exactly! It's also super cute—sometimes it sleeps by your feet, and sometimes it climbs all over you.

Liu Xiaoxue: You've really taken great care of it. Staff: It feels like it's our own child. We take care of it, and now it knows us.

Liu Xiaoxue: Mom, can we get a kitten?

Wang Yixue: We don't have time to take care of one. How about I take you to the Beijing Zoo this weekend to see the animals instead?

##### 课文2

Liu Xiaoming: Mom, the Chinese name "giant panda" sounds so strange. What do they have to do with cats?

Wang Yixue: That's a great question. They have nothing to do with cats. Actually, they should be called "Giant Cat-Bears".

Liu Xiaoming: Why do giant pandas only eat bamboo and not meat?

bamboo and not meat?

Wang Yixue: They can eat meat, just like us, but they're simply not interested in it.

Liu Xiaoming: Why are giant pandas considered China's national treasure?

Wang Yixue: China is the only country with wild giant pandas—it's their natural home.

Liu Xiaoming: I heard some pandas have been sent abroad. Where did they go?

Wang Yixue: That's a long story! I'll have to tell you about it bit by bit.

Liu Xiaoxue: Little brother, you're like a walking “One Hundred Thousand Whys”.

##### 课文3

Liu Xiaoxue: This giant panda keeps climbing up and down—it's so adorable!

Wang Yixue: It seems like it's still hungry.

Look, it's standing up with its mouth open,

trying to find something to eat.

Liu Xiaoxue: The panda next to it hasn't moved in a while. Is it sleeping?

Wang Yixue: I can't see its face, so maybe it is.

You know, pandas usually nap a lot during the day.

Liu Xiaoxue: Right, and when they are not sleeping, they spend most of their time eating.

Liu Xiaoming: What a relaxing life!

Liu Xiaoxue: Mom, can you take a picture of me with these two giant pandas?

##### 课文4

Today, I took Xiaoxue and Xiaoming to the zoo. The Beijing Zoo is one of the most famous zoos in China and is home to a wide variety of animals. Of course, the giant pandas are favorites among both adults and children. Every day, many visitors flock to the Panda House, bringing their cameras to take photos of these adorable animals. The zoo opens at 7:30 a.m. and closes at 7:00 p.m. In winter, there are fewer visitors than in summer, so it closes an hour earlier. If it's your first time visiting Beijing, a trip to the Beijing Zoo with your family is definitely a great choice.

#### Lesson 17

#### 我要多向认真的人学习

##### 课文1

Liu Xiaoxue: Let's stop here. We've already reached Ms. Li's office.

Classmate: You go in. I'll wait for you by the stairs. I'm scared to see Ms. Li.

Liu Xiaoxue: What's the matter?

Classmate: I’ve made quite a few mistakes recently, and I’m afraid Ms. Li will be upset. Liu Xiaoxue: You’re overthinking it. Everyone makes mistakes.

Classmate: But I got some simple things wrong.

Ms. Li must think I'm not careful.

Liu Xiaoxue: Some people, even though they're very smart, still make mistakes on simple tasks because they think the tasks are easy and don't take them seriously.

Classmate: That sounds just like me. From now on, I'll learn from people who are careful.

##### 课文2

Bai Jiayue: Has the sun risen in the west?

I have been seeing you in the library almost every day lately.

Chen Tianzhong: What's so strange about that?

I come here to study.

Bai Jiayue: Why are you still studying in the library? Everyone else is busy looking for jobs!

Chen Tianzhong: I'm not ready to start working yet—I want to keep studying, and my parents agree with me. But I haven't decided what to study, so I'm coming here to see what's available.

Bai Jiayue: Don't you like computers? You could check out some books about them. Chen Tianzhong: Good idea. I remember seeing some computer books in that room—I'll go and have a look.

##### 课文3

Li Wen: Tianzhong, why is your phone off?

Chen Tianzhong: My phone ran out of battery.

Is something wrong?

Li Wen: I saw Jiayue the day before yesterday,

and she told me you're planning to continue your studies abroad.

Chen Tianzhong: Yes, but I haven't decided what to study yet, let alone which country to go to.

Li Wen: Why not go to China to study Chinese?

Big cities like Beijing and Shanghai have many excellent universities and lots of international students.

Chen Tianzhong: Sounds good. I'll check it out online.

Li Wen: I can recommend a few useful websites to you.

##### 课文4

My university life is about to end, and I've

often been asked recently, “What do you want to do after graduation?”—a question that’s truly not easy to answer. I’ve asked several classmates too, and each has chosen a different path. Some want to pursue graduate studies, some plan to study abroad, and others have decided to look for a job first. I believe only by figuring out what you truly enjoy can you make the best decision. For me, however, finding the answer to “what I truly enjoy” remains a tough question.

#### Lesson 18 我学会了包饺子

##### 课文1

Bai Jiayue: I'm going to spend Spring Festival in China. I'm so excited!

Li Wen: This is the first time I've invited a foreign friend to my home for the Spring Festival. I'm really happy too.

Bai Jiayue: How do you usually celebrate the festival?

Li Wen: Spring Festival is the most important holiday in China. We usually go back home and celebrate it with our families.

Bai Jiayue: And what do you do with your family?

Li Wen: We make jiaozi while watching the Spring Festival Gala, and we stay up past midnight.

Bai Jiayue: That sounds really fun. How many days off do you get for Spring Festival?

Li Wen: About seven or eight days.

##### 课文2

Bai Jiayue: Auntie Zhang, Uncle Li—Happy New Year! I brought a gift for you. I hope you like it.

Uncle Li: Thank you, Jiayue. That's very kind of you!

Aunt Zhang: Xiaowen always talks about you. I'm so glad we finally get to meet.

Bai Jiayue: Thank you for having me over.

Uncle Li: There are drinks and bottled water on the table—help yourself to whatever you'd like.

Aunt Zhang: Have a seat and watch some TV for a bit. The jiaozi will be ready soon.

Bai Jiayue: I learned how to make jiaozi from a video. Let me help you make them!

##### 课文3

Bai Jiayue: Was this photo taken before Li Wen went abroad?

Aunt Zhang: Yes, it was taken at the airport in Beijing when our whole family saw him off.

Bai Jiayue: That was about three or four years ago, right?

Aunt Zhang: Yes, time really flies—it feels like it happened just yesterday.

Bai Jiayue: You must really miss Li Wen, don't you?

Aunt Zhang: When he first left home, it was hard for me to get used to it. If I didn't video chat with him for a few days, I'd miss him terribly.

Bai Jiayue: Well, just one more semester until he graduates. He'll be back soon.

##### 课文4

What made me happiest during this trip to China was getting to know Li Wen's parents. They showed me photos taken when Li Wen was born and told me many stories about his childhood. In high school, he wanted to study medicine and has worked hard ever since. In his senior year, his outstanding grades earned him the opportunity to study abroad. Through his story, I learned that as long as you persevere, you can achieve your goals. I believe he will go on to accomplish even greater things in the future.
